  document
    .getElementById("footer-privacy-choices")
    ?.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (e.currentTarget.dataset.consentModal === "zaraz") {
        window.zaraz?.showConsentModal();
      } else {
        window.Osano?.cm?.showDrawer();
      }
    });
